{"forms":[{"form":"adverbial particles","tags":["plural"]}],"lang_code":"en","pos":"noun","senses":[{"examples":[{"text":"In the phrasal verbs 'set up', 'throw away', 'shut down', 'carry out' and 'take over', the words 'up', 'away', 'down', 'out' and 'over' are the adverbial particles.","type":"example"},{"text":"The sentence 'He turns on everyone he meets' has very different meanings depending on whether the word 'on' is parsed as a preposition (i.e. turn on as \"to attack or criticise suddenly\") or as an adverbial particle (i.e. turn on as \"arouse\").","type":"example"}],"glosses":["The adverbial component of a phrasal verb; a word, typically a short one, which functions as an adverb accompanying and qualifying the verb component of a phrasal verb."],"topics":["human-sciences","linguistics","sciences"]}],"upstream_line":7421409,"upstream_offset":16684967528,"upstream_record_sha256":"07839a5c1af9031ec986f280837584fa95fb7c0101dd52a0ac79e31efdd7eb02","word":"adverbial particle"}
